###### The Wholesome Triad ^7-1-1-1-4-0

####### *The Dependence Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-1-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-1-0

######## *Positive – The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-2-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a wholesome state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on an unwholesome state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-53

######## *The Object Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-3-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the object condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the object condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the object condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates. (1) ^7-2-54

######## *The Predominance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-4-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a wholesome state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the predominance condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on an unwholesome state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the predominance condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-55

######## *The Proximity and Contiguity Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-5-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the proximity condition… because of the contiguity condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates. (The proximity and contiguity conditions are the same as the object condition.) ^7-2-56

######## *The Co-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-6-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the co-arising condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the co-arising condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (3)

(1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the co-arising condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the co-arising condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-57

(1) ^7-2-58

######## *The Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-8-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the support condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate…. (The support condition is the same as the co-arising condition.) ^7-2-59

######## *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-9-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the strong-support condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate…. (The strong-support condition is the same as the object condition.) ^7-2-60

######## *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-10-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. The physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. The physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. The physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1) ^7-2-61

Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)

Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state….

For the physical base, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. Dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the presence condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates.

######## *The Absence and Disappearance Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-20-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the absence condition… because of the disappearance condition. (The absence condition and the disappearance condition are the same as the object condition.) ^7-2-71

######## *The Non-disappearance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-2-21-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the non-disappearance condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates. (The non-disappearance condition is the same as the co-arising condition).

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-1-0

######## *Negative – The Not-Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-2-0

Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, the delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1) ^7-2-86

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a wholesome state, wholesome and neutral states arise not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on an unwholesome state, unwholesome and neutral states arise not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma. Dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates. Dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-88

######## *The Not-Proximity and Not-Contiguity Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-5-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the proximity condition… not because of the contiguity condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (The not-proximity condition and the not-contiguity condition are the same as the not-object condition.) ^7-2-89

######## *The Not-Mutuality Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-6-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the mutuality condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the mutuality condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the mutuality condition – dependent on the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the resultant neutral aggregates, matter produced by kamma; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; dependent on the external great elements, derived matter; dependent on the great elements produced by nutriment, derived matter; dependent on the great elements produced by temperature, derived matter; among non-percipient beings, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the mutuality condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the mutuality condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-90

######## *The Not-Strong-Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-7-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the strong-support condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (The not-strong-support condition is the same as the not-object condition.) ^7-2-91

######## *The Not-Pre-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-8-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. Dependent on an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates; dependent on the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma. Dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates. Dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-92

######## *The Not-Post-arising and Not-Repetition Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-9-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the post-arising condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate….
Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate…. (The not-post-arising condition and the not-repetition condition are the same as the not-predominance condition.) ^7-2-93

######## *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-10-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, wholesome volition. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates, unwholesome volition. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the functional neutral aggregates, functional neutral volition; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature, dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, derived matter. (1) ^7-2-94

######## *The Not-Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-11-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the result condition – dependent on one wholesome aggregate… three.
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the result condition… three.
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the result condition – dependent on one functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter.
Dependent on a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the result condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness.
Dependent on an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the result condition – dependent on the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. ^7-2-95

######## *The Not-Nutriment Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-12-0

Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the nutriment condition – external… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1) ^7-2-96

######## *The Not-Faculty Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-13-0

Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the faculty condition – external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, derived matter; among non-percipient beings, dependent on the great elements, the material life faculty. (1) ^7-2-97

######## *The Not-Jhāna Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-14-0

Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the jhāna condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by the five kinds of sense-consciousness, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1) ^7-2-98

######## *The Not-Path Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-15-0

Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the path condition – dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma. Dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates. Dependent on one great element, three great elements; dependent on three great elements, one great element; dependent on two great elements, two great elements; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1) ^7-2-99

######## *The Not-Association Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-16-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the association condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (The same as the not-object condition.) ^7-2-100

######## *The Not-Dissociation Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-4-17-0

Dependent on a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Dependent on an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Dependent on a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; dependent on three aggregates, one aggregate; dependent on two aggregates, two aggregates; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element, three great elements… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. Dependent on a wholesome state, a neutral state arises not because of the absence condition… not because of the disappearance condition – dependent on the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. (The same as the not-object condition.) ^7-2-102

####### *The Co-arising Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-8-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-9-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-9-1-0

######## *The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-9-2-0

Co-arising with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates. Co-arising with a wholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising with a wholesome state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – co-arising with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-234

Co-arising with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates. Co-arising with an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising with an unwholesome state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – co-arising with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-235

Co-arising with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, co-arising with one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; co-arising with three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; co-arising with two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; co-arising with the aggregates, the physical base; co-arising with the physical base, the aggregates; co-arising with one great element, three great elements; co-arising with three great elements, one great element; co-arising with two great elements, two great elements; co-arising with the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1) ^7-2-236

Co-arising with a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Co-arising with an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – co-arising with the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)

######## *The Predominance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-4-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the predominance condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the predominance condition – with one unwholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the predominance condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the root condition.) ^7-2-253

######## *The Proximity and Contiguity Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-5-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the proximity condition… because of the contiguity condition.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the object condition.) ^7-2-254

######## *The Co-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-6-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the co-arising condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the co-arising condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… at the moment of rebirth… with one great element as condition… external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the co-arising condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the root condition.) ^7-2-255

######## *The Mutuality Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-7-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the mutuality condition… one.
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the mutuality condition… one.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the mutuality condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, with one resultant neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and the physical base… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and the physical base; with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; with one great element as condition, three great elements… with two great elements as condition, two great elements; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition, three great elements… with two great elements as condition, two great elements; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the mutuality condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the object condition.) ^7-2-256

######## *The Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-8-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the support condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the co-arising condition.) ^7-2-257

######## *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-9-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the strong-support condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition. (The same as the object condition.) ^7-2-258

######## *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-10-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition. With one wholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates. The physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with one unwholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates. The physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition.
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with the physical base as condition, the unwholesome aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (3)
With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with one wholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – with one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1) ^7-2-259

######## *The Repetition Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-11-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition….
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – with one unwholesome aggregate as condition….
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the repetition condition – with one functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates; with three aggregates as condition, one aggregate; with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates; with the physical base as condition, the functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – with the physical base as condition, the unwholesome aggregates.
With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition….
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the repetition condition – with one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates…. ^7-2-260

######## *The Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-12-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the kamma condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the kamma condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the kamma condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition… at the moment of rebirth… with one great element as condition… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the kamma condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the kamma condition – with the physical base as condition, the unwholesome aggregates…. (5)
With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state… a neutral state… wholesome and neutral states arise because of the kamma condition….
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state… a neutral state… unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the kamma condition – with one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition… with the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. ^7-2-261

######## *The Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-13-0

With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the result condition. With one resultant neutral aggregate as condition… at the moment of rebirth… with one great element as condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral aggregates. ^7-2-262

######## *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-14-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the nutriment condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the nutriment condition… at the moment of rebirth… one great element produced by nutriment… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. (Complete.) ^7-2-263

######## *The Faculty Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-15-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the faculty condition… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
(The faculty condition should be expanded in the same way as the kamma condition.) ^7-2-264

######## *The Jhāna and Path Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-16-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the jhāna condition… because of the path condition.
(The jhāna condition and the path condition should both be expanded in the same way as the root condition.) ^7-2-265

######## *The Association Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-17-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the association condition. (The same as the object condition.) ^7-2-266

######## *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-18-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition. With one wholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. With a wholesome state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the dissociation condition – with the wholesome aggregates as condition, matter produced by consciousness; the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. With a wholesome state as condition, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (3)
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition – with one unwholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. With an unwholesome state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the dissociation condition – with the unwholesome aggregates as condition, matter produced by consciousness; the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. With an unwholesome state as condition, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with one unwholesome aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (3)
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the dissociation condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition; at the moment of rebirth, with one resultant neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by kamma, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition; with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for the physical base, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition; with one great element as condition… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter – the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. (2)
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition – with the physical base as condition, the unwholesome aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. (3)
With a neutral state as condition, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (4)
With a neutral state as condition, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with the physical base as condition, the unwholesome aggregates; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. For matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (5)
With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition. With one wholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates; with three aggregates and the physical base as condition, one aggregate; with two aggregates and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the dissociation condition. With the wholesome aggregates and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. The aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with one wholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates; with three aggregates and the physical base as condition, one aggregate; with two aggregates and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with the wholesome aggregates and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (3)
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition – with one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises because of the dissociation condition – with the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the dissociation condition – with one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates; with the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; for the aggregates, the physical base, because of the dissociation condition; for matter produced by consciousness, the aggregates, because of the dissociation condition. (3) ^7-2-267

######## *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-15-19-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises because of the presence condition… (the presence condition should be treated as the same as the co-arising condition. The absence condition and the disappearance condition are the same as the object condition; the non-disappearance condition is the same as the co-arising condition.) ^7-2-268

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-0

######## *The Counting* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-1-0

######## *Single Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-2-0

Root: seventeen, object: seven, predominance: seventeen, proximity: seven, contiguity: seven, co-arising: seventeen, mutuality: seven, support: seventeen, strong-support: seven, pre-arising: seven, repetition: seven, kamma: seventeen, result: one, nutriment: seventeen, faculty: seventeen, jhāna: seventeen, path: seventeen, association: seven, dissociation: seventeen, presence: seventeen, absence: seven, disappearance: seven, non-disappearance: seventeen. ^7-2-269

######## *Pairs with Root* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-3-0

With root as condition – object: seven, predominance: seventeen, proximity: seven, contiguity: seven, co-arising: seventeen… non-disappearance: seventeen. ^7-2-270

######## *Threes* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-4-0

With root and object as conditions – predominance: seven, (seven in every case) result: one, non-disappearance: seven…. ^7-U6

######## *Twelves (with repetition)* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-5-0

With root, object, predominance, proximity, contiguity, co-arising, mutuality, support, strong-support, pre-arising and repetition as conditions – kamma: seven, nutriment: seven… non-disappearance: seven…. ^7-U7

######## *Twenty-twos (with repetition)* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-6-0

With root, object… pre-arising, repetition, kamma, nutriment… disappearance as conditions – non-disappearance: seven. ^7-U8

######## *Thirteens (with result)* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-7-0

With root, object… pre-arising, kamma and result as conditions – nutriment: one… non-disappearance: one. ^7-U9

######## *Twenty-twos (with result)* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-8-0

With root, object… pre-arising, kamma, result, nutriment… disappearance as conditions – non-disappearance: one. The series based on the root condition. ^7-U10

######## *Pairs with Object* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-9-0

With object as condition – root: seven, predominance: seven… (the series based on the object condition should be expanded in the same way as the series based on the root condition.) ^7-2-271

######## *Pairs with Predominance* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-10-0

With predominance as condition – root: seventeen…. ^7-2-272

######## *Pairs with Proximity and Contiguity* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-11-0

With proximity as condition, with contiguity as condition – root: seven…. ^7-2-273

######## *Pairs with Co-arising and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-12-0

With co-arising as condition… with mutuality as condition… with support as condition… with strong-support as condition… with pre-arising as condition….
With repetition as condition – root: seven, object: seven, predominance: seven, proximity: seven, contiguity: seven, co-arising: seven, mutuality: seven, support: seven, strong-support: seven, pre-arising: seven, kamma: seven, nutriment: seven, faculty: seven, jhāna: seven, path: seven, association: seven, dissociation: seven, presence: seven, absence: seven, disappearance: seven, non-disappearance: seven…. ^7-2-274

######## *Pairs with Kamma and Result* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-13-0

With kamma as condition… with result as condition – root: one, object: one, predominance: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, pre-arising: one, kamma: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, jhāna: one, path: one, association: one, dissociation: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one…. ^7-2-275

######## *Pairs with Nutriment and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-16-14-0

With nutriment as condition… with faculty as condition… with jhāna as condition… with path as condition… with association as condition… with dissociation as condition… with presence as condition, with absence as condition… with disappearance as condition….
With non-disappearance as condition – root: seventeen, object: seven… disappearance: seven.
The positive method in the Condition Section. ^7-2-276

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-1-0

######## *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-2-0

With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the root condition – with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, with one resultant neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. With the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates without roots. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) ^7-2-277

######## *The Not-Object Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-3-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the object condition – with the wholesome aggregates as condition, matter produced by consciousness.
(It should be expanded in the same way as the not-object condition in the dependence section.) ^7-2-278

######## *The Not-Predominance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-4-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the predominance condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition… at the moment of rebirth… (the neutral should be given in full). External… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates….
(It should be counted in the same way as the co-arising condition in the positive method.) ^7-2-279

######## *The Not-Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-5-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the proximity condition… not because of the contiguity condition… not because of the mutuality condition… not because of the strong-support condition… not because of the pre-arising condition.
(It should be expanded in the same way as in the dependence section.) ^7-2-280

######## *The Not-Post-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-6-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the post-arising condition… not because of the repetition condition… with the eye base as condition… (the not-post-arising condition and the not-repetition condition are both complete: seventeen.)
(It should be expanded in the same way as the co-arising condition in the positive method.) ^7-2-281

######## *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-7-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the wholesome aggregates as condition, wholesome volition. (1)
With an unwholesome state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the unwholesome aggregates as condition, unwholesome volition. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the functional neutral aggregates as condition, functional neutral volition; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature, with one great element as condition… derived matter; with the physical base as condition, functional neutral volition. (1)
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the physical base as condition, wholesome volition. (2)
With a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the physical base as condition, unwholesome volition. (3)
With a wholesome and a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the wholesome aggregates and the physical base as condition, wholesome volition. (1)
With an unwholesome and a neutral state as condition, an unwholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition – with the unwholesome aggregates and the physical base as condition, unwholesome volition. (1) ^7-2-282

######## *The Not-Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-8-0

With a wholesome state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the result condition – with one wholesome aggregate as condition… three.
With an unwholesome state as condition… three.
With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the result condition – with one functional neutral aggregate as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; with the physical base as condition, the functional neutral aggregates.
With a neutral state as condition, a wholesome state arises not because of the result condition – with the physical base as condition, the wholesome aggregates.
(Leaving out the resultant, it should be expanded in every case.) ^7-2-283

######## *The Not-Nutriment Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-9-0

With a neutral state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the nutriment condition… not because of the faculty condition… not because of the jhāna condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness. (Under not-jhāna, this is the difference.) Not because of the path condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness. With the physical base as condition, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates without roots. (Under not-path, this is the difference.)
(The rest should be expanded in the same way as the negative method in the dependence section.) ^7-2-284

######## *The Not-Association Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-17-10-0

Not because of the association condition… not because of the dissociation condition… not because of the absence condition… with a wholesome state as condition, a neutral state arises not because of the disappearance condition – with the wholesome aggregates as condition, matter produced by consciousness.
(It should be expanded in the same way as in the dependence section.) ^7-2-285

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-18-0

######## *The Counting* ^7-1-1-1-4-18-1-0

######## *Single Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-18-2-0

Not-root: four, not-object: five, not-predominance: seventeen, not-proximity: five, not-contiguity: five, not-mutuality: five, not-strong-support: five, not-pre-arising: seven, not-post-arising: seventeen, not-repetition: seventeen, not-kamma: seven, not-result: seventeen, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: five, not-dissociation: three, not-absence: five, not-disappearance: five. ^7-2-286

######## *Pairs with Not-Root* ^7-1-1-1-4-18-3-0

With not-root as condition – not-object: one, not-predominance: four, not-proximity: one, not-contiguity: one, not-mutuality: one, not-strong-support: one, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: four, not-repetition: four, not-kamma: one, not-result: four, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: one, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: one, not-disappearance: one. ^7-2-287

######## *Threes* ^7-1-1-1-4-18-4-0

####### *The Support Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-21-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-22-0

####### *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-23-0

######## *The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-23-1-0

Supported by a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; supported by three aggregates, one aggregate; supported by two aggregates, two aggregates. Supported by a wholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – supported by the wholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Supported by a wholesome state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition. Supported by one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; supported by three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-329

Supported by an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates… supported by two aggregates, two aggregates. Supported by an unwholesome state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – supported by the unwholesome aggregates, matter produced by consciousness. Supported by an unwholesome state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – supported by one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-330

Supported by a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – supported by one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; supported by three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, supported by one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma; supported by three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by kamma; supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; supported by the aggregates, the physical base; supported by the physical base, the aggregates; supported by one great element, three great elements; supported by three great elements, one great element; supported by two great elements, two great elements; supported by the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; supported by the physical base, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. (1)
Supported by a neutral state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by the physical base, the wholesome aggregates. (2)
Supported by a neutral state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by the physical base, the unwholesome aggregates. (3)
Supported by a neutral state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – supported by the physical base, the wholesome aggregates; supported by the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (4)
Supported by a neutral state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – supported by the physical base, the unwholesome aggregates; supported by the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (5) ^7-2-331

Supported by a wholesome and a neutral state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by one wholesome aggregate and the physical base, three aggregates… supported by two aggregates and the physical base, two aggregates. Supported by a wholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – supported by the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. Supported by a wholesome and a neutral state, wholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – supported by one wholesome aggregate and the physical base, three aggregates… supported by two aggregates and the physical base, two aggregates; supported by the wholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (3)
Supported by an unwholesome and a neutral state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – supported by one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base, three aggregates… supported by two aggregates and the physical base, two aggregates. Supported by an unwholesome and a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – supported by the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. Supported by an unwholesome and a neutral state, unwholesome and neutral states arise because of the root condition – supported by one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base, three aggregates… supported by two aggregates and the physical base, two aggregates; supported by the unwholesome aggregates and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (3)
(It should be expanded as in the Condition Section.) ^7-2-332

Root: seventeen, object: seven, predominance: seventeen, proximity: seven, contiguity: seven, co-arising: seventeen, mutuality: seven, support: seventeen, strong-support: seven, pre-arising: seven, repetition: seven, kamma: seventeen, result: one, nutriment: seventeen, faculty: seventeen, jhāna: seventeen, path: seventeen, association: seven, dissociation: seventeen, presence: seventeen, absence: seven, disappearance: seven, non-disappearance: seventeen.

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-26-0

####### *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-27-0

######## *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-27-1-0

Supported by an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – supported by the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Supported by a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the root condition – supported by one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; supported by three aggregates, one aggregate and matter produced by consciousness; supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, supported by one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… supported by two aggregates, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; supported by the aggregates, the physical base; supported by the physical base, the aggregates; supported by one great element, three great elements… supported by the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, supported by one great element, three great elements… supported by the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; supported by the eye base, eye-consciousness… supported by the body base, body-consciousness; supported by the physical base, the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates without roots. (1)
Supported by a neutral state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – supported by the physical base, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
Supported by an unwholesome and a neutral state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – supported by the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and by the physical base, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
(It should be expanded as in the Condition Section.) ^7-2-334

####### *The Conjoined Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-32-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-1-0

######## *The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-2-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Conjoined with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, conjoined with one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1) ^7-2-338

######## *The Object Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-3-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the object condition… because of the predominance condition… (At the moment of rebirth there is no predominance.) Because of the proximity condition… because of the contiguity condition… because of the co-arising condition… because of the mutuality condition… because of the support condition… because of the strong-support condition. (All the items are the same as those based on the root condition.) ^7-2-339

######## *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-4-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – conjoined with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
Conjoined with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the pre-arising condition – conjoined with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1)
Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the pre-arising condition – conjoined with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the pre-arising condition. (1) ^7-2-340

######## *The Repetition Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-5-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state… with an unwholesome state… conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the repetition condition – conjoined with one functional neutral aggregate…. ^7-2-341

######## *The Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-6-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the kamma condition… conjoined with an unwholesome state… conjoined with a neutral state…. ^7-2-342

######## *The Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-7-0

Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the result condition – resultant neutral…. ^7-2-343

######## *The Nutriment Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-8-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the nutriment condition… because of the faculty condition… because of the jhāna condition… because of the path condition… because of the association condition. (These items are the same as the root condition.) ^7-2-344

######## *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-9-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the dissociation condition – conjoined with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; the physical base, because of the dissociation condition.
Conjoined with an unwholesome state… the physical base, because of the dissociation condition.
Conjoined with a neutral state… the physical base, because of the dissociation condition. ^7-2-345

######## *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-33-10-0
