####### *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-0

######## *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-1-0

Conjoined with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, conjoined with one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates; conjoined with three aggregates, one aggregate; conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1) ^7-2-351

######## *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-2-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the predominance condition… not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm [variant: āruppe (sabbattha)], conjoined with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. Conjoined with an unwholesome state… conjoined with a neutral state…. ^7-2-352

######## *The Not-Post-arising and Not-Repetition Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-3-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the post-arising condition… three. Not because of the repetition condition… three. ^7-2-353

######## *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-4-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition. Conjoined with the wholesome aggregates, wholesome volition. (1)
Conjoined with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the kamma condition. Conjoined with the unwholesome aggregates, unwholesome volition. (1)
Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the kamma condition. Conjoined with the functional neutral aggregates, functional neutral volition. (1) ^7-2-354

######## *The Not-Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-5-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state… with an unwholesome state… conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the result condition – one functional neutral aggregate.
(In the exposition of the negative method in the Conjoined Section, there is no rebirth under not-kamma and not-result; in all the remaining ones it is present.) ^7-2-355

######## *The Not-Jhāna Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-6-0

Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the jhāna condition – conjoined with one aggregate accompanied by the five kinds of sense-consciousness, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1) ^7-2-356

######## *The Not-Path Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-7-0

Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the path condition – conjoined with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (1) ^7-2-357

######## *The Not-Dissociation Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-37-8-0

Conjoined with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, conjoined with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Conjoined with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, conjoined with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1)
Conjoined with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, conjoined with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates. (Under not-dissociation there is no rebirth.) (1) ^7-2-358

With not-result, not-root, not-predominance and not-pre-arising as conditions – object: two, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: two, mutuality: two, support: two, strong-support: two, repetition: one, kamma: two, nutriment: two, faculty: two, jhāna: two, path: one, association: two, presence: two, absence: two, disappearance: two, non-disappearance: two.
(In the series based on not-result, this is the difference; the rest are as in the series based on not-root.) ^7-U114

######## *Pairs with Not-Jhāna* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-14-0

With not-jhāna as condition – object: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, pre-arising: one, kamma: one, result: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, association: one, dissociation: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one…. ^7-2-389

######## *Sevens* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-15-0

With not-jhāna, not-root, not-predominance, not-post-arising, not-repetition and not-path as conditions – object: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, pre-arising: one, kamma: one, result: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, association: one, dissociation: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one. ^7-U115

######## *Pairs with Not-Path* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-16-0

With not-path as condition – object: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, pre-arising: one, repetition: one, kamma: one, result: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, jhāna: one, association: one, dissociation: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one…. ^7-2-390

######## *Fives* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-17-0

With not-path, not-root, not-predominance and not-pre-arising as conditions – object: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, kamma: one, result: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, jhāna: one, association: one, dissociation: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one. ^7-U116

######## *Sixes and the Rest* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-18-0

With not-path and not-root as conditions. (Abbreviated.) ^7-U117

######## *Pairs with Not-Dissociation* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-19-0

With not-dissociation as condition – root: three, object: three, predominance: three, proximity: three, contiguity: three, co-arising: three, mutuality: three, support: three, strong-support: three, repetition: three, kamma: three, result: one, nutriment: three, faculty: three, jhāna: three, path: three, association: three, presence: three, absence: three, disappearance: three, non-disappearance: three. ^7-2-391

######## *Threes* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-20-0

With not-dissociation and not-root as conditions – object: two, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: two, mutuality: two, support: two, strong-support: two, repetition: one, kamma: two, nutriment: two, faculty: two, jhāna: two, path: one, association: two, presence: two, absence: two, disappearance: two, non-disappearance: two…. ^7-U118

######## *Nines* ^7-1-1-1-4-40-21-0

With not-dissociation, not-root, not-predominance, not-pre-arising and not-post-arising as conditions (the series based on not-repetition too is the same as the series based on not-root.) With not-kamma, not-result and not-path as conditions (these three bases are alike) – object: one, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: one, mutuality: one, support: one, strong-support: one, nutriment: one, faculty: one, jhāna: one, association: one, presence: one, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: one.
The negative-positive method.
The Conjoined Section. ^7-U119

####### *The Association Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-41-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-42-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-42-1-0

######## *The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-42-2-0

Associated with a wholesome state, a wholesome state arises because of the root condition – associated with one wholesome aggregate, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1) ^7-2-392

Associated with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises because of the root condition – associated with one unwholesome aggregate, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates. (1) ^7-2-393

Associated with a neutral state, a neutral state arises because of the root condition – associated with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, associated with one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates. (Abbreviated.) (1) ^7-2-394

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-43-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-1-1-1-4-44-0

######## *Single Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-44-1-0

Root: three, object: three, predominance: three, proximity: three, contiguity: three, co-arising: three, mutuality: three, support: three, strong-support: three, pre-arising: three, repetition: three, kamma: three, result: one, nutriment: three, faculty: three, jhāna: three, path: three, association: three, dissociation: three, presence: three, absence: three, disappearance: three, non-disappearance: three.
The positive method. ^7-2-395

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-45-0

####### *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-46-0

######## *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-46-1-0

Associated with an unwholesome state, an unwholesome state arises not because of the root condition – associated with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) ^7-2-396

Associated with a neutral state, a neutral state arises not because of the root condition – associated with one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate without roots, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, associated with one resultant neutral aggregate, three aggregates; associated with three aggregates, one aggregate; associated with two aggregates, two aggregates. (Abbreviated.) (1) ^7-2-397

####### *Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-47-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-1-1-1-4-48-0

######## *Single Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-48-1-0

Not-root: two, not-predominance: three, not-pre-arising: three, not-post-arising: three, not-repetition: three, not-kamma: three, not-result: three, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: three. (Abbreviated.)
The negative method. ^7-2-398

####### *Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-49-0

######## *Pairs with Root* ^7-1-1-1-4-49-1-0

With root as condition – not-predominance: three, not-pre-arising: three, not-post-arising: three, not-repetition: three, not-kamma: three, not-result: three, not-dissociation: three. (Abbreviated.)
The positive-negative method. ^7-2-399

####### *Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-50-0

######## *Pairs with Not-Root* ^7-1-1-1-4-50-1-0

With not-root as condition – object: two, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: two, mutuality: two, support: two, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: two, result: one, nutriment: two, faculty: two, jhāna: two, path: one, association: two, dissociation: two, presence: two, absence: two, disappearance: two, non-disappearance: two. (Abbreviated.)
The negative-positive method.
The Association Section.
(Being conjoined is the same as being associated, and being associated is the same as being conjoined.) ^7-2-400

####### *The Analysis Section* ^7-1-1-1-4-51-0

####### *Positive Method of the Conditions* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-0

######## *The Exposition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-1-0

######## *The Root Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-2-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of root condition – wholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates. A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of root condition – wholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of root condition – wholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-401

An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of root condition – unwholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates. An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of root condition – unwholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of root condition – unwholesome roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-402

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of root condition – resultant neutral and functional neutral roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, resultant neutral roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (1) ^7-2-403

######## *The Object Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-3-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of object condition – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it; one reviews good deeds done in the past; having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna; trainees review change-of-lineage, they review cleansing; trainees, having emerged from the path, review the path; trainees or ordinary people see the wholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self; by knowledge of others' minds they know the consciousness of one who possesses wholesome consciousness. The wholesome state of the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of object condition, for the wholesome state of the base of infinite consciousness. The wholesome state of the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of object condition, for the wholesome state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception. Wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma and knowledge of the future. (1) ^7-2-404

A wholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. One enjoys and delights in good deeds done in the past; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. Having emerged from jhāna, one enjoys and delights in the jhāna; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises. When the jhāna has been lost, distress arises in one who feels remorse. (2) ^7-2-405

A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition – an arahant, having emerged from the path, reviews the path; reviews good deeds done in the past; sees the wholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self; by knowledge of others' minds knows the consciousness of one who possesses wholesome consciousness. Trainees or ordinary people see the wholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self; when the wholesome has ceased, a result arises as registration. One enjoys and delights in the wholesome; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises; when the unwholesome has ceased, a result arises as registration. The wholesome state of the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of object condition, for the resultant and the functional states of the base of infinite consciousness. The wholesome state of the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of object condition, for the resultant and the functional states of the base of neither perception nor non-perception. Wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and adverting. (3) ^7-2-406

An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition – one enjoys and delights in lust; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. One enjoys and delights in wrong view; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. With doubt as object, doubt arises, wrong view arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. With restlessness as object, restlessness arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, distress arises. With distress as object, distress arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises. (1) ^7-2-407

An unwholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of object condition – trainees review abandoned defilements [variant: pahīnakilese (Syā.)], they review suppressed defilements [variant: vikkhambhitakilese (Syā.)], they know the defilements they habitually indulged in before; trainees or ordinary people see the unwholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self; by knowledge of others' minds they know the consciousness of one who possesses unwholesome consciousness. Unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma and knowledge of the future. (2) ^7-2-408

An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition – an arahant reviews abandoned defilements, knows the defilements habitually indulged in before, sees the unwholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, by knowledge of others' minds knows the consciousness of one who possesses unwholesome consciousness; trainees or ordinary people see the unwholesome with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self; when the wholesome has ceased, a result arises as registration. One enjoys and delights in the unwholesome; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. When the unwholesome has ceased, a result arises as registration. Unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and adverting. (3) ^7-2-409

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition – an arahant reviews fruit, reviews nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for fruit and for adverting. An arahant sees the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… the physical base… sees the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element, by knowledge of others' minds knows the consciousness of one who possesses resultant neutral or functional neutral consciousness. The functional state of the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of object condition, for the functional state of the base of infinite consciousness. The functional state of the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of object condition, for the functional state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness. The sound base for ear-consciousness… the odour base for nose-consciousness… the taste base for tongue-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of object condition, for body-consciousness. Neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of the future and adverting. (1) ^7-2-410

A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of object condition – trainees review fruit, they review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing and the path. Trainees or ordinary people see the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… the physical base… they see the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, see a visible form with the divine eye, hear a sound with the divine ear element, by knowledge of others' minds know the consciousness of one who possesses resultant neutral or functional neutral consciousness. Neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives and knowledge of the future. ^7-2-411

A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition – one enjoys and delights in the eye; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… the physical base… one enjoys and delights in the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. (3) ^7-2-412

######## *The Predominance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-4-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, giving it weight; one reviews good deeds done in the past, giving them weight; having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, giving it weight. Trainees review change-of-lineage, giving it weight; they review cleansing, giving it weight. Trainees, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight. Co-arising predominance – wholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1) ^7-2-413

A wholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one enjoys and delights in it, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. One enjoys and delights in good deeds done in the past, giving them weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Having emerged from jhāna, one enjoys and delights in the jhāna, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises.
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – an arahant, having emerged from the path, reviews the path, giving it weight. Co-arising predominance – wholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – wholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (4) ^7-2-414

An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – one enjoys and delights in lust, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. One enjoys and delights in wrong view, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – unwholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – unwholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – unwholesome predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-2-415

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – an arahant reviews fruit, giving it weight; reviews nibbāna, giving it weight. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for fruit. Co-arising predominance – resultant neutral and functional neutral predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – trainees review fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing and for the path. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – one enjoys and delights in the eye, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… the physical base… one enjoys and delights in the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates, giving them weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (3) ^7-2-416

######## *The Proximity Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-5-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of proximity condition – preceding wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following wholesome aggregates. Conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the path. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of proximity condition. The wholesome for emergence… the path for fruit… conformity for a trainee's attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the wholesome state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of proximity condition – preceding unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following unwholesome aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of proximity condition. The unwholesome is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of proximity condition – preceding resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. The life-continuum for adverting… the functional for emergence… for an arahant, conformity for the attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the functional state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of proximity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of proximity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3) ^7-2-417

######## *The Contiguity Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-6-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of contiguity condition – preceding wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for following wholesome aggregates. Conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for the path.
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of contiguity condition. The wholesome for emergence… the path for fruit… conformity for a trainee's attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the wholesome state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of contiguity condition – preceding unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for following unwholesome aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of contiguity condition – the unwholesome is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for emergence. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of contiguity condition – preceding resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for following resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. The life-continuum for adverting… the functional for emergence… for an arahant, conformity for the attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the functional state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of contiguity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of contiguity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of contiguity condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3) ^7-2-418

######## *The Co-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-7-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of co-arising condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of co-arising condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by kamma. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. The aggregates are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for the physical base. The physical base is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for the aggregates. One great element is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. One external great element is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for derived matter. One great element produced by nutriment is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for derived matter. One great element produced by temperature is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for derived matter. Among non-percipient beings, one great element is a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – wholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
An unwholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of co-arising condition – unwholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of co-arising condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-2-419

######## *The Mutuality Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-8-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of mutuality condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of mutuality condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of mutuality condition – one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two aggregates. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three aggregates and for the physical base. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for one aggregate and for the physical base. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two aggregates and for the physical base. The aggregates are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for the physical base. The physical base is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for the aggregates. One great element is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two great elements; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, one great element is a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for three great elements… two great elements are a condition, by way of mutuality condition, for two great elements. (1) ^7-2-420

######## *The Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-9-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of support condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of support condition – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of support condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of support condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of support condition – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of support condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of support condition – one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by kamma. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. The aggregates are a condition, by way of support condition, for the physical base. The physical base is a condition, by way of support condition, for the aggregates. One great element is a condition, by way of support condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, one great element is a condition, by way of support condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by kamma and derived matter. The eye base is a condition, by way of support condition, for eye-consciousness. The ear base… the nose base… the tongue base… the body base is a condition, by way of support condition, for body-consciousness. The physical base is a condition, by way of support condition, for resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates.
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of support condition – the physical base is a condition, by way of support condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of support condition – the physical base is a condition, by way of support condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3) ^7-2-421

A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a wholesome state by way of support condition – one wholesome aggregate and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of support condition – wholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome and a neutral state are a condition for an unwholesome state by way of support condition – one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of support condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of support condition – unwholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of support condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2) ^7-2-422

######## *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-10-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, giving it weight; one reviews good deeds done in the past, giving them weight; having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, giving it weight; trainees review change-of-lineage, giving it weight; they review cleansing, giving it weight; trainees, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight.
Proximity strong-support – preceding wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for following wholesome aggregates. Conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the path.
Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Relying on virtue… on learning… on generosity… on wisdom, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Faith… virtue… learning… generosity… wisdom… is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for virtue… for learning… for generosity… for wisdom.
The preliminary work for the first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the first jhāna. The preliminary work for the second jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second jhāna. The preliminary work for the third jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the third jhāna. The preliminary work for the fourth jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth jhāna. The preliminary work for the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite space. The preliminary work for the base of infinite consciousness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite consciousness. The preliminary work for the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of nothingness. The preliminary work for the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. The first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second jhāna. The second jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the third jhāna. The third jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth jhāna. The fourth jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite space. The base of infinite space is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite consciousness. The base of infinite consciousness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of nothingness. The base of nothingness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. The preliminary work for the divine eye is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the divine eye. The preliminary work for the divine ear element is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the divine ear element. The preliminary work for knowledge of supernormal powers is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers. The preliminary work for knowledge of others' minds is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of others' minds. The preliminary work for knowledge of recollecting past lives is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of recollecting past lives. The preliminary work for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma. The preliminary work for knowledge of the future is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of the future. The divine eye is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the divine ear element. The divine ear element is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers. Knowledge of supernormal powers is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of others' minds. Knowledge of others' minds is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of recollecting past lives. Knowledge of recollecting past lives is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma. Knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of the future. The preliminary work for the first path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the first path. The preliminary work for the second path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second path. The preliminary work for the third path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the third path. The preliminary work for the fourth path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. The first path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second path. The second path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the third path. The third path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. Relying on the path, trainees produce an attainment that has not yet arisen, enter one that has arisen, and see formations with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for trainees' analytical knowledge of meaning, analytical knowledge of teachings, analytical knowledge of language, analytical knowledge of ready expression, and for skill in what is possible and what is impossible. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts and carrying out the observance-day duties, one enjoys and delights in it, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. One enjoys and delights in good deeds done in the past, giving them weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Having emerged from jhāna, one enjoys and delights in the jhāna, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises.
Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view. Relying on virtue… on learning… on generosity… on wisdom, one fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view. Faith… virtue… learning… generosity… wisdom is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for hatred… for delusion… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – an arahant, having emerged from the path, reviews the path, giving it weight.
Proximity strong-support – the wholesome for emergence… the path for fruit… conformity for a trainee's attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the wholesome state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition.
Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one torments and afflicts oneself, and undergoes suffering rooted in seeking. Relying on virtue… on learning… on generosity… on wisdom, one torments and afflicts oneself, and undergoes suffering rooted in seeking. Faith… virtue… learning… generosity… wisdom is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the attainment of fruition. Wholesome kamma is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for its result. Relying on the path, an arahant produces a functional attainment that has not yet arisen, enters one that has arisen, and sees formations with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for an arahant's analytical knowledge of meaning, analytical knowledge of teachings, analytical knowledge of language, analytical knowledge of ready expression, and for skill in what is possible and what is impossible. The path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – one enjoys and delights in lust, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. One enjoys and delights in wrong view, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises.
Proximity strong-support – preceding unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for following unwholesome aggregates.
Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one kills a living being, takes what is not given, tells lies, speaks divisively, speaks harshly, chatters idly, breaks into houses, carries off plunder, robs a single house, lies in ambush on the road, goes to another's wife, raids a village, raids a town, takes the life of one's mother, takes the life of one's father, takes the life of an arahant, with a malicious mind draws the Buddha's blood, causes a split in the monastic community. Relying on hatred… relying on delusion… relying on conceit… relying on wrong view… relying on longing, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community. Lust… hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for hatred… for delusion… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing. Killing living beings is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for killing living beings. Killing living beings is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for taking what is not given… for sexual misconduct… for false speech… for divisive speech… for harsh speech… for idle chatter… for covetousness… for ill will… for wrong view. Taking what is not given is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for taking what is not given… for sexual misconduct… for false speech… (abbreviated) for wrong view… for killing living beings. (The cycle should be linked together.) Sexual misconduct… false speech… divisive speech… harsh speech… idle chatter… covetousness… ill will… wrong view is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for wrong view. Wrong view is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for killing living beings… for taking what is not given… for sexual misconduct… for false speech… for divisive speech… for harsh speech… for idle chatter… for covetousness… for ill will. The act of killing one's mother is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing one's mother. The act of killing one's mother is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing one's father… for the act of killing an arahant… for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… for the act of splitting the monastic community… for fixed wrong view. The act of killing one's father is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing one's father… for the act of killing an arahant… for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… for the act of splitting the monastic community… for fixed wrong view… for the act of killing one's mother. The act of killing an arahant is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing an arahant… for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… the act of drawing the Buddha's blood for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… the act of splitting the monastic community for the act of splitting the monastic community… fixed wrong view is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for fixed wrong view. Fixed wrong view is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing one's mother… for the act of killing an arahant… for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… for the act of splitting the monastic community. (The cycle should be worked out.) (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of strong-support condition. Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Relying on hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Lust… hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for virtue… for learning… for generosity… for wisdom. Having killed a living being, in order to counteract it one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Having taken what is not given… having told lies… having spoken divisively… having spoken harshly… having chattered idly… having broken into a house… having carried off plunder… having robbed a single house… having lain in ambush on the road… having gone to another's wife… having raided a village… having raided a town, in order to counteract it one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Having taken the life of one's mother, in order to counteract it one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties. Having taken the life of one's father… having taken the life of an arahant… having drawn the Buddha's blood with a malicious mind… having caused a split in the monastic community, in order to counteract it one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Proximity strong-support – the unwholesome is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for emergence.
Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one torments and afflicts oneself, and undergoes suffering rooted in seeking. Relying on hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing, one torments and afflicts oneself, and undergoes suffering rooted in seeking. Lust… hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the attainment of fruition. Unwholesome kamma is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for its result. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – an arahant reviews fruit, giving it weight; reviews nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for fruit.
Proximity strong-support – preceding resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for following resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. The life-continuum for adverting… the functional for emergence… for an arahant, conformity for the attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the functional state of the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition.
Natural strong-support – bodily pleasure is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. Bodily pain is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. Climate is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. Food is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. Lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. Bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain and for the attainment of fruition. The attainment of fruition is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure.
Relying on bodily pleasure, an arahant produces a functional attainment that has not yet arisen, enters one that has arisen, and sees formations with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. Relying on bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, an arahant produces a functional attainment that has not yet arisen, enters one that has arisen, and sees formations with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – trainees review fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for change-of-lineage… for cleansing… for the path.
Proximity strong-support – adverting is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for wholesome aggregates.
Natural strong-support – relying on bodily pleasure, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Relying on bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment. Bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for virtue… for learning… for generosity… for wisdom. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.
Object strong-support – one enjoys and delights in the eye, giving it weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… the physical base… one enjoys and delights in the resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates, giving them weight; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises.
Proximity strong-support – adverting is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for unwholesome aggregates.
Natural strong-support – relying on bodily pleasure, one kills a living being, takes what is not given, tells lies, speaks divisively, speaks harshly, chatters idly, breaks into houses, carries off plunder, robs a single house, lies in ambush on the road, goes to another's wife, raids a village, raids a town, takes the life of one's mother, takes the life of one's father, takes the life of an arahant, with a malicious mind draws the Buddha's blood, causes a split in the monastic community.
Relying on bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, one kills a living being… (Abbreviated.) One causes a split in the monastic community.
Bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for hatred… for delusion… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing. (3) ^7-2-423

######## *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-11-0

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base.
Pre-arising object – an arahant sees the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… sees the physical base with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness. The sound base for ear-consciousness… the odour base for nose-consciousness… the taste base for tongue-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness.
Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness. The ear base for ear-consciousness… the nose base for nose-consciousness… the tongue base for tongue-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. The physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base.
Pre-arising object – trainees or ordinary people see the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… they see the physical base with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self. They see a visible form with the divine eye. They hear a sound with the divine ear element.
Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – one enjoys and delights in the eye; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… one enjoys and delights in the physical base; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises.
Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3) ^7-2-424

######## *The Post-arising Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-12-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of post-arising condition – post-arising wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of post-arising condition – post-arising unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of post-arising condition – post-arising resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1) ^7-2-425

######## *The Repetition Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-13-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of repetition condition – preceding wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of repetition condition, for following wholesome aggregates. Conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing is a condition, by way of repetition condition, for the path. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of repetition condition – preceding unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of repetition condition, for following unwholesome aggregates. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of repetition condition – preceding functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of repetition condition, for following functional neutral aggregates. (1) ^7-2-426

######## *The Kamma Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-14-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of kamma condition – wholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment [variant: nānākhaṇikā (Ka.)]. Co-arising – wholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness.
From a different moment – wholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of kamma condition – wholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of kamma condition – unwholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – unwholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. From a different moment – unwholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of kamma condition – unwholesome volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of kamma condition – resultant neutral and functional neutral volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, resultant neutral volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. Volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the physical base. (1) ^7-2-427

######## *The Result Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-15-0

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of result condition – one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of result condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of result condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of result condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of result condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of result condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by kamma. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of result condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. The aggregates are a condition, by way of result condition, for the physical base. (1) ^7-2-428

######## *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-16-0

Physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body. (1) ^7-2-429

######## *The Faculty Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-17-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of faculty condition – wholesome faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of faculty condition – wholesome faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of faculty condition – unwholesome faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of faculty condition – unwholesome faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of faculty condition – unwholesome faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)

At the moment of rebirth, resultant neutral faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. The eye faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for eye-consciousness. The ear faculty for ear-consciousness… the nose faculty for nose-consciousness… the tongue faculty for tongue-consciousness… the body faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for body-consciousness. The material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1) ^7-2-430

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of jhāna condition – wholesome jhāna factors are a condition, by way of jhāna condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)

An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of jhāna condition – unwholesome jhāna factors are a condition, by way of jhāna condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of jhāna condition – unwholesome jhāna factors are a condition, by way of jhāna condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of jhāna condition – resultant neutral and functional neutral jhāna factors are a condition, by way of jhāna condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. ######## *The Association Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-20-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of association condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of association condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of association condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of association condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of association condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of association condition, for three aggregates. A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of association condition – one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of association condition, for three aggregates. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of association condition, for three aggregates.
