######## *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-21-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, resultant neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by kamma. The aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base. The physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness. The ear base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for ear-consciousness. The nose base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for nose-consciousness. The tongue base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for tongue-consciousness. The body base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for body-consciousness. The physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. Post-arising – resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of dissociation condition – pre-arising: the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of dissociation condition – pre-arising: the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3) ^7-2-434

######## *The Presence Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-22-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of presence condition – one wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – wholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. (2)
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of presence condition. One wholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of presence condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates. An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – unwholesome aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. (2)
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of presence condition – one unwholesome aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one resultant neutral or functional neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by consciousness. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. At the moment of rebirth, one resultant neutral aggregate is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. Three aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate and for matter produced by kamma. Two aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. The aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the physical base. The physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates. One great element is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. External… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature… among non-percipient beings, one great element is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three great elements. Three great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one great element. Two great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two great elements. The great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma and derived matter.
Pre-arising – an arahant sees the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self… the ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… sees the physical base with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, sees a visible form with the divine eye; hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness. The sound base… the tangible base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness. The eye base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness. The ear base for ear-consciousness… the nose base for nose-consciousness… the tongue base for tongue-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness. The physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates. Post-arising – resultant neutral and functional neutral aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Physical food is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. The material life faculty is a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of presence condition. Pre-arising: trainees or ordinary people see the eye with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self… the ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… see the physical base with insight as impermanent, as suffering, as not-self, see a visible form with the divine eye, hear a sound with the divine ear element. The physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for wholesome aggregates. (2)
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of presence condition – pre-arising: one enjoys and delights in the eye; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises. The ear… the nose… the tongue… the body… visible forms… sounds… odours… tastes… tangible objects… one enjoys and delights in the physical base; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises. The physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for unwholesome aggregates. (3)
A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a wholesome state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one wholesome aggregate and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates. (1)
A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – wholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – wholesome aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising wholesome aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
An unwholesome and a neutral state are a condition for an unwholesome state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one unwholesome aggregate and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates. Three aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for one aggregate. Two aggregates and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates. (1)
An unwholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a neutral state by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – unwholesome aggregates and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – unwholesome aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – unwholesome aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-2-435

######## *The Non-disappearance Condition* ^7-1-1-1-4-52-25-0

(It should be expanded in the same way as the presence condition.)
The exposition of the Analysis Section. ^7-2-438

######## *(2) Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-1-1-1-4-54-89-0

A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition.
A wholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition.
A wholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition.
A wholesome state is a condition for a wholesome and a neutral state by way of co-arising condition. (4) ^7-2-527

An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition.

An unwholesome state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition.
An unwholesome state is a condition for an unwholesome and a neutral state by way of co-arising condition. (4) ^7-2-528

A neutral state is a condition for a neutral state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition.
A neutral state is a condition for a wholesome state by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition.
A neutral state is a condition for an unwholesome state by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (3) ^7-2-529

A wholesome and a neutral state are a condition for a wholesome state: co-arising, pre-arising.
